# Multi-environment and matched-label evaluation

Generated by `python scripts/multi_env_eval.py data/processed/cicids2017.parquet data/processed/csecicids2018.parquet --model lgbm --cap 60000 --shots 10,50,200 --seeds 42,1 --local-benign 1000 --calibration-fraction 0.2 --results docs/results/multi_env_matched.md --target-only --lab data/lab/run_20261025T051149Z/flows_labelled.csv --benign-eval data/lab/run_20261025T051701Z/flows_labelled.csv data/lab/run_20261025T051751Z/flows_labelled.csv` on 2026-10-29.

Training is capped per class; test partitions are full. Target labels are oracle-balanced draws from earlier per-class partitions, not first incidents. Seeds vary label selection; model seed stays fixed.

Matched conditions share exact target rows and reserve benign calibration inside the annotation budget. They use no source validation or extra target validation. Legacy baselines retain disclosed validation access. Matched results are not interchangeable with historical source-calibrated few-shot results.
